Bengaluru, Dated: 03-02-2023

NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of notification No. RD 12 LRD 2019, dated
08.11.2022, regarding alteration of the limits of Bhalki and Humnabad
taluks of Bidar District by excluding the certain villages specified in
the schedules from the said taluks and by including said villages in the
limits of Hulsoor taluk of Bidar District was published as required by
section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12
of 1964) in Part IV-A of the Karnataka Gazette, dated: 17-11-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within one month from the date of its publication in
the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to the public
on 17-11-2022.
And whereas objections and suggestions have been received in this
behalf and considered by the State Government.
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-
section (4) of section 4 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(4165)

SOLE BOF LE TW, BHA, OY, Aww, 9092 eon Ya
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964), the Government of Karnataka hereby
makes the following notification, namely:-

The Villages specified in column (3) of the table below shall be
excluded from the limits of taluk specified in column (2) and shall be
included within the limits of taluk specified in column (4) thereof:-

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 972 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:22-12-2017 regarding altering the limits of Kanivenahalli village of
Pavagada Taluk and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called
Ivarlahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 972
KAMGRARA 2017 Dated:22-03-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all

the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:22-
10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below and areas specified in column(3) threr of
shall be excluded from the limits of said village Kanivenahalli of Pavagada Taluk
and Tumkur District to form a new revenue village called Ivarlahalli of Pavagada
Taluk in Tumkur district along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 357 PFH 2022
Dated:15-11-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Karikere village of Tiptur Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting
hamlet/extention called Anchekoppalu within the boundary of Karikere revenue
village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 357 PFH 2022
Dated:17-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated:18-11-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
wihin the limits of Karikere revenue village, namely:- Anchekoppalu

SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Anchekoppalu village of Tiptur Taluk of
Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the boundary
of Karikere Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Anchekoppalu
of Karikere village in Tiptur Taluk of tumkuru District.

with Survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Anchekoppalu
hamlet/extention part of Karikere village in Tiptur taluk of Tumkuru District.
The above shall come into effect from date of publication of final notification
in the official gazette.

By order in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

(Dr. Rajendra Prasad M. N.,)
Director,
Cell for creation of revenue villages
and Ex-officio Joint Secretary to Govt.
Revenue Department

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 974 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:22-01-2018 regarding altering the limits of Kondethimmanahalli village of
Pavagada Taluk and Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue village called
Ramayannapalya was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
974 KAMGRARA 2017 Dated:22-03-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 05-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

specified in column (2) of the table bellow and areas specified in column (3) there of
shall be exclude from limits of said village Kondethimmanahalli of Pavagada taluk
in Tumkuru District to form a new Revenue village called as Ramayyana Palya of
Pavagada taluk Tumkuru district along with the survey numbers included in column
(4)there of.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 103 GLH 2022 Dated:17-06-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Nalkudure (G) village of Channagiri Taluk and
Davanagere District and constituting a new revenue sub-village called
Basaveshwara Nagara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
103 GLH 2022 Dated:11-07-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:11-
07-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely.
SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Nalkudure village of Channagiri Taluk in Davangere District to from a new
revenue village called as Basaveshwara Nagara of Channagiri Taluk and
Davangere District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 99 KAMGRARA 2018
Dated:28-09-2018 regarding altering the limits of Pothaganahalli village of
Pavagada Taluk and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called
Indrabetta and Rudrayyanaroppa was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka
Gazette No. RD 99 KAMGRARA 2018 Dated: 08-11-2018 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:19-
10-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below and areas specified in column(3) threr of
shall be excluded from the limits of said village Pothaganahalli of Pavagada Taluk
and Tumkur District to form a new revenue village called Indrabetta of Pavagada
Taluk in Tumkur district along with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 231 GLH 2022 Dated:
05-12-2022 regarding altering the limits of Vaddaradibba village of Tarikere Taluk
in Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a new revenue village called
Indiranagara, was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 231 GLH
2022 Dated:07.12.2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely
to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
07-12-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Indiranagara village Tarikere talluk of
Chikkamagaluru district and convert the same to form hamlet extension within the
boundary of Vaddaradibba revenue village and same will here in after be named as
Indiranagara of Vaddaradibba village Tarikere talluk, Chikkamagaluru district.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(4) above with survey
numbers specified in column(3) on which the said unrecorded habitation is located is
proposed to be converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of Indiranagara
hamlet/extension/part of Vaddaradibba village in Tarikere talluk of Chikkamagaluru
district.
The above shall come into effect from date of publication of final notification in the
official gazette.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD139 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits Of Yardona Village of Kartagi taluk Koppal
District and constituting a new revenue village called Ramachandrapura was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD139 PFH 2022

dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated.17-10-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Yardona of Kartagi taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue village
called Ramachandrapura of Kartagi taluk Koppal district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Bengaluru Date:13-02-2023

PRILIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (IV) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of The Village, The demand under Land Revenue in The
Village and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of 73-Hulikunte Village
of Sandur Taluk, Ballari District and to constitute a New Village Called
Uttaramalai as Specified in Schedule along With Maps With Old Survey
Numbers and New Survey Numbers assigned to Them.

Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that The proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the Date of Publication of the Notification in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari Disrict, Ballari.

SCHEDULE –1
Lands bearing Survey Numbers of The Villages and Survey Numbers of The
areas Specified in Column (2) of The Table below Shall be Excluded from The
limits of Said Villages of Ballari District Sandur Taluk to form a New Revenue
Village called as Uttaramalai Village of Sandur taluk, Ballari district along with the
survey numbers included in Column (4) There of.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 27 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:13/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Angarekanahalli village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 15 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:13-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Byadarahalli Village of Krishnarajanagar taluk Mysore District and to constitute a
new revenue village called Siddanakopalu as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Mysore District, Mysore

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Byadarahalli Village of Krishnarajanagar taluk Mysore District to form a
new revenue village called Siddanakopalu of Krishnarajanagar taluk Mysore
district along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-313
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 18 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:13/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Chlakayalaparti village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Chlakayalaparti village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Chlakayalaparti village
of Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Chlakayalaparti Revenue village and
same will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Chlakayalaparti village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue

specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Hiriyannanahalli village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk,
Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule
below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Hiriyannanahalli village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 299 PFH 2022 Dated:15-11-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Hulkihal village of Karatagi Taluk and Koppal
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shreenagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 299 PFH 2022 Dated:16-11-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:17-
11-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

village Hulkihal of karatagi Taluk and Koppal District to form a new revenue
village called shreenagar of karatagi taluk in Koppal district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-324

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 36 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:13/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Kalavara village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Kalavara village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkaballapura District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in column (3) of the table
shall be declared as habitation area of Kalavara village of Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District convert the same to form extension within the boundary of
Kalavara revenue village and same will be herein after be named as Kalavara village
in Chikkaballapura Taluk Chikkaballapur district.

PRILIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (IV) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of The Village, The demand under Land Revenue in The
Village and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kalingeri Village of
Sandur Taluk, Ballari District and to constitute a New Village Called Dodda
Upparahalli as Specified in Schedule along With Maps With Old Survey Numbers
and New Survey Numbers assigned to Them.

Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that The proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the Date of Publication of the Notification in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari Disrict, Ballari.

PR-326
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 28 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat,
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:13-02-2023

PRILIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (IV) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of The Village, The demand under Land Revenue in The
Village and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kalingeri Village of
Sandur Taluk, Ballari District and to constitute a New Village Called Hiral as
Specified in Schedule along With Maps With Old Survey Numbers and New
Survey Numbers assigned to Them.

Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that The proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the Date of Publication of the Notification in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari Disrict, Ballari.

SCHEDULE –1

Lands bearing Survey Numbers of The Villages and Survey Numbers of The
areas Specified in Column (2) of The Tablebelow Shall be Excluded from The limits
of Said Villages of Ballari District Sandur Taluk to form a New Revenue Village
called as Hiral Village of Sandur Taluk, Ballari District along with the Survey
Numbers Included in Column (4) There of

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

PRILIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (IV) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of The Village, The demand under Land Revenue in The
Village and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kanakapura Village
of Sandur Taluk, Ballari District and to constitute a New Village Called
Devarlahalli as Specified in Schedule along With Maps With Old Survey
Numbers and New Survey Numbers assigned to Them.

Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that The proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the Date of Publication of the Notification in the Official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Ballari Disrict, Ballari.

SCHEDULE –1
Lands bearing Survey Numbers of The Villages and Survey Numbers of The
areas Specified in Column (2) of The Table below Shall be Excluded from The
limits of Said Villages of Ballari District Sandur Taluk to form a New Revenue
Village called as Devarlahalli Village of Sandur Taluk, Ballari District along with
the Survey Numbers Included in Column (4) There of

PR-328
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 11 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:13-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Kattariguppe village in Kasaba Hobli of Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura
District to extend the original gramatana extent of the existing revenue village. The
area occupied by the said village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Kattariguppe village for extension of original gramatana extent of the said revenue
village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Kattariguppe village of
Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and convert the same to form
habitation extension within the boundary of Kattariguppe Revenue village and same
will be named as part of original gramatana extent of Kattariguppe village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

Hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(3) above with
survey numbers specified in column(2) in which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be extended as part of original gramatana extent, habitation area
(Gramatana), of Kattariguppe village of Kasaba Hobli, Chikkaballapura Taluk of
Chikkaballapura District.

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnatanka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964).

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act No 12 of 1964)and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clasuses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue

in the village and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Kenchenahalli village
of Arasikere taluk, Hassan district and to constitute a new villages called Kuvempu
Nagara as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new
survey numbers assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected there
by that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan.

PR-330
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 07 PFH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:13-02-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Kodadur Village of Kalagi Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing
Kodadur Tanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing

revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in
Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of Kodadur village
for conversion of Kodadur Tanda unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part
of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act, notice
is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby that
the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from any
persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period specified
above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions may be
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kodadur village of Kalagi Taluk of
Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Kodadur Tanda(upanagar) Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Guruji Nagar Tanda(Upanagar) of Kodadur villagein Kalagi Taluk of Kalaburagi
District.

and Pandarapura Village Tuppadahalli and Kulamigatte Village

The above shall come into effect from date of publication of final notification
in the official gazette.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 48 GLH 2023 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru. Date:20/02/2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Avulahalli, Marenahalli & Honnappanahalli village in Kasaba Hobli of
Chikkaballapura Taluk, Chikkaballapura District to extend the original gramatana
extent of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Avulahalli, Marenahalli & Honnappanahalli village for extension of original
gramatana extent of the said revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chikkballapura district.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers in column (2) and extent specified below in
column(3) of the table shall be declared as habitation area of Avulahalli, Marenahalli
& Honnappanahalli village of Chikkaballpura taluk of Chikkaballpura district and
convert the same to form habitation extension within the boundary of Avulahalli,
Marenahalli & Honnappanahalli Revenue village and same will be named as part of
original gramatana extent of Avulahalli, Marenahalli & Honnappanahalli village
Chikkaballpura Taluk, Chikkaballpura District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 101 GLH 2022 Dated:17-
06-2022 regarding altering the limits of Bistuvalli village of Jagalur Taluk and
Davanagere District and constituting a new revenue village called Devagiri was

published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 101 GLH 2022
Dated:08-07-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:16-
08-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely.
SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Bistuvalli village of Jagalur Taluk in Davanagere district to from a new revenue
village called as Devagiri of Jagalur Taluk and Davanagere district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
